+# --- §7 Follow-up matcher (find_followup_news) -----------------------------
+# 07:00 ET on 2026-05-05 (EDT) == 11:00 UTC.
+
+
+def _followup_halt(symbol="VRDN"):
+    return HaltEvent(
+        symbol=symbol, exchange="Nasdaq", halt_date="2026-05-05",
+        halt_time="07:00:00", reason_code="T1", reason_description="News Pending",
+        name="Viridian Therapeutics", last_price=14.06, source="nasdaq_rss",
+    )
+
+
+def test_find_followup_matches_pr_after_halt():
+    halt = _followup_halt()
+    cache = NewsCache()
+    now = datetime(2026, 5, 5, 11, 10, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
+    cache.ingest([NewsItem(
+        source="prnewswire", title="Viridian reports positive Phase 3 topline",
+        body="b", url="https://prn.test/vrdn",
+        published_at="2026-05-05T11:10:00+00:00", tickers=("VRDN",),
+    )], now_utc=now)
+    item = find_followup_news(halt, cache)
+    assert item is not None
+    assert item.title == "Viridian reports positive Phase 3 topline"
+
+
+def test_find_followup_ignores_pr_before_halt():
+    """A PR that crossed BEFORE the halt is the cross-ref-note case, not a
+    follow-up — find_followup_news requires publish time AT/AFTER the halt."""
+    halt = _followup_halt()
+    cache = NewsCache()
+    now = datetime(2026, 5, 5, 11, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
+    cache.ingest([NewsItem(
+        source="prnewswire", title="pre-halt PR", body="b",
+        url="https://prn.test/pre", published_at="2026-05-05T10:50:00+00:00",
+        tickers=("VRDN",),
+    )], now_utc=now)
+    assert find_followup_news(halt, cache) is None
+
+
+def test_find_followup_picks_earliest_when_multiple():
+    """Among multiple post-halt PRs, the EARLIEST at/after the halt is the
+    breaking one that resolved it (tie-break opposite of the cross-ref note)."""
+    halt = _followup_halt()
+    cache = NewsCache()
+    now = datetime(2026, 5, 5, 11, 40, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
+    cache.ingest([
+        NewsItem(source="businesswire", title="later unrelated wire", body="b",
+                 url="https://x.test/late", published_at="2026-05-05T11:35:00+00:00",
+                 tickers=("VRDN",)),
+        NewsItem(source="prnewswire", title="breaking PR that resolved the halt",
+                 body="b", url="https://x.test/break",
+                 published_at="2026-05-05T11:05:00+00:00", tickers=("VRDN",)),
+    ], now_utc=now)
+    item = find_followup_news(halt, cache)
+    assert item.title == "breaking PR that resolved the halt"
+
+
+def test_find_followup_no_match_returns_none():
+    halt = _followup_halt()
+    assert find_followup_news(halt, NewsCache()) is None  # empty cache
+
+
+def test_find_followup_skips_item_without_title():
+    halt = _followup_halt()
+    cache = NewsCache()
+    now = datetime(2026, 5, 5, 11, 10, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
+    cache.ingest([NewsItem(
+        source="prnewswire", title="   ", body="b", url="https://prn.test/empty",
+        published_at="2026-05-05T11:10:00+00:00", tickers=("VRDN",),
+    )], now_utc=now)
+    assert find_followup_news(halt, cache) is None
+
+
+def test_find_followup_reuses_halt_dt_conversion_returns_none_on_bad_halt():
+    """Malformed halt time flows through the shared _halt_dt_to_utc → None."""
+    bad = HaltEvent(
+        symbol="VRDN", exchange="Nasdaq", halt_date="", halt_time="",
+        reason_code="T1", reason_description="News Pending", source="nasdaq_rss",
+    )
+    cache = NewsCache()
+    now = datetime(2026, 5, 5, 11, 10, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
+    cache.ingest([NewsItem(
+        source="prnewswire", title="a real PR", body="b", url="https://prn.test/x",
+        published_at="2026-05-05T11:10:00+00:00", tickers=("VRDN",),
+    )], now_utc=now)
+    assert find_followup_news(bad, cache) is None
+
+

diff --git a/tests/test_followup.py b/tests/test_followup.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..6cca079
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/test_followup.py
@@ -0,0 +1,214 @@
+"""Tests for §7 Follow-up detection + emission (_emit_followups + delivery gate).
+
+Covers the correctness-sensitive lifecycle rules:
+- a follow-up fires ONLY for a halt sa-monitor actually DELIVERED an alert for
+  (emitted_halts), not one merely observed (seen_halts)
+- non-emit reason code (never alerted)      → no follow-up  (Bug 1)
+- live post_halt that failed (never posted)  → no follow-up  (Bug 2)
+- resolving PR already cached at halt time    → cross-ref note, NO duplicate
+  standalone follow-up                                        (Bug 3)
+- resolving PR that arrives in a LATER poll   → STILL fires a follow-up
+- one follow-up per halt, ever; no-match → nothing; dry-run posts+marks nothing
+"""
+from datetime import datetime, timezone
+from unittest.mock import patch
+
+from src.coverage import TickerMeta
+from src.dedup import HaltTracker
+from src.feeds.types import HaltEvent
+from src.halt_monitor import RunStats, _emit, _emit_followups
+from src.news.cache import NewsCache
+from src.news.types import NewsItem
+
+
+class StubUniverse:
+    """Minimal Universe stand-in — _emit / _emit_followups only call .get()."""
+
+    def __init__(self, mapping):
+        self._m = {k.upper(): v for k, v in mapping.items()}
+
+    def get(self, symbol):
+        return self._m.get(symbol.upper())
+
+
+def _halt(symbol="VRDN", reason_code="T1"):
+    # 07:00 ET on 2026-05-05 (EDT) == 11:00 UTC.
+    return HaltEvent(
+        symbol=symbol, exchange="Nasdaq", halt_date="2026-05-05",
+        halt_time="07:00:00", reason_code=reason_code,
+        reason_description="News Pending", name="Viridian Therapeutics",
+        last_price=14.06, source="nasdaq_rss",
+    )
+
+
+def _meta(symbol="VRDN"):
+    return TickerMeta(symbol=symbol, name="Viridian Therapeutics Inc",
+                      sector="Biopharma", subsector="Biotech")
+
+
+def _post_halt_pr_cache(pub="2026-05-05T11:10:00+00:00", symbol="VRDN",
+                        title="Viridian reports positive Phase 3 topline"):
+    cache = NewsCache()
+    now = datetime(2026, 5, 5, 11, 40, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
+    cache.ingest([NewsItem(
+        source="prnewswire", title=title, body="b",
+        url=f"https://prn.test/{symbol}", published_at=pub, tickers=(symbol,),
+    )], now_utc=now)
+    return cache
+
+
+def _deliver_halt(tracker, event, universe, cache, *, slack_mode="off",
+                  include_non_emit=False, stats=None):
+    """Run the REAL halt-delivery path (tracker.ingest → _emit) so emitted_halts
+    is populated with production's exact delivery semantics."""
+    stats = stats or RunStats(started_at="t")
+    for kind, ev in tracker.ingest([event]):
+        _emit(kind, ev, universe, None, include_non_emit=include_non_emit,
+              stats=stats, slack_mode=slack_mode, news_cache=cache, tracker=tracker)
+    return stats
+
+
+def _run_followups(tracker, universe, cache, *, slack_mode="off"):
+    stats = RunStats(started_at="t")
+    _emit_followups(tracker, universe, cache, None,
+                    stats=stats, slack_mode=slack_mode)
+    return stats
+
+
+def test_delivered_covered_halt_then_later_pr_emits_followup(capsys):
+    halt = _halt()
+    universe = StubUniverse({"VRDN": _meta()})
+    tracker = HaltTracker()
+    _deliver_halt(tracker, halt, universe, NewsCache())  # delivered, no PR yet
+    assert halt.halt_id in tracker.emitted_halts
+
+    stats = _run_followups(tracker, universe, _post_halt_pr_cache())
+    assert stats.followups_emitted == 1
+    assert halt.halt_id in tracker.followed_up
+    out = capsys.readouterr().out
+    assert "Follow-up: Viridian reports positive Phase 3 topline" in out
+    assert "Follows the 07:00 ET halt on VRDN" in out
+
+
+def test_one_followup_per_halt_ever():
+    halt = _halt()
+    universe = StubUniverse({"VRDN": _meta()})
+    tracker = HaltTracker()
+    _deliver_halt(tracker, halt, universe, NewsCache())
+    cache = _post_halt_pr_cache()
+
+    first = _run_followups(tracker, universe, cache)
+    second = _run_followups(tracker, universe, cache)  # same state, PR still cached
+    assert first.followups_emitted == 1
+    assert second.followups_emitted == 0
+    assert len(tracker.followed_up) == 1
+
+
+def test_no_match_no_emit():
+    halt = _halt()
+    universe = StubUniverse({"VRDN": _meta()})
+    tracker = HaltTracker()
+    _deliver_halt(tracker, halt, universe, NewsCache())
+    # PR crossed BEFORE the halt — the cross-ref-note case, not a follow-up.
+    cache = NewsCache()
+    cache.ingest([NewsItem(
+        source="prnewswire", title="pre-halt PR", body="b",
+        url="https://prn.test/pre", published_at="2026-05-05T10:50:00+00:00",
+        tickers=("VRDN",),
+    )], now_utc=datetime(2026, 5, 5, 11, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc))
+
+    stats = _run_followups(tracker, universe, cache)
+    assert stats.followups_emitted == 0
+    assert tracker.followed_up == set()
+
+
+# --- Bug 1: non-emit reason code (never alerted) → no follow-up -------------
+def test_non_emit_reason_code_halt_no_followup():
+    halt = _halt(reason_code="O1")  # Operations Halt — excluded by default
+    universe = StubUniverse({"VRDN": _meta()})
+    tracker = HaltTracker()
+    _deliver_halt(tracker, halt, universe, NewsCache())  # skipped by non-emit filter
+    assert halt.halt_id in tracker.seen_halts          # observed
+    assert halt.halt_id not in tracker.emitted_halts   # but NOT delivered
+
+    stats = _run_followups(tracker, universe, _post_halt_pr_cache())
+    assert stats.followups_emitted == 0
+    assert tracker.followed_up == set()
+
+
+# --- Bug 2: live post_halt failed (never posted) → no follow-up -------------
+@patch("src.slack.post_halt", side_effect=RuntimeError("slack down"))
+def test_failed_live_post_halt_no_followup(_mock_post):
+    halt = _halt()
+    universe = StubUniverse({"VRDN": _meta()})
+    tracker = HaltTracker()
+    _deliver_halt(tracker, halt, universe, NewsCache(), slack_mode="live")
+    assert halt.halt_id in tracker.seen_halts
+    assert halt.halt_id not in tracker.emitted_halts  # post raised → not delivered
+
+    stats = _run_followups(tracker, universe, _post_halt_pr_cache())
+    assert stats.followups_emitted == 0
+    assert tracker.followed_up == set()
+
+
+# --- Bug 3: PR cached at halt time → cross-ref note, NO duplicate follow-up --
+def test_pr_at_halt_time_yields_note_not_duplicate_followup(capsys):
+    halt = _halt()
+    universe = StubUniverse({"VRDN": _meta()})
+    tracker = HaltTracker()
+    # PR coincident with the halt instant (11:00:00Z), cached BEFORE delivery.
+    cache = _post_halt_pr_cache(pub="2026-05-05T11:00:00+00:00")
+    _deliver_halt(tracker, halt, universe, cache)
+
+    out = capsys.readouterr().out
+    assert "Follows PR Newswire press release" in out  # halt's cross-ref note
+    assert halt.halt_id in tracker.emitted_halts
+    assert halt.halt_id in tracker.followed_up  # pre-marked → suppresses standalone
+
+    stats = _run_followups(tracker, universe, cache)
+    assert stats.followups_emitted == 0  # no duplicate
+
+
+# --- The complement: PR that lands in a LATER poll STILL fires --------------
+def test_pr_in_later_poll_still_fires_followup():
+    halt = _halt()
+    universe = StubUniverse({"VRDN": _meta()})
+    tracker = HaltTracker()
+    _deliver_halt(tracker, halt, universe, NewsCache())  # empty cache at delivery
+    assert halt.halt_id not in tracker.followed_up  # not pre-marked
+
+    stats = _run_followups(tracker, universe, _post_halt_pr_cache())  # PR now present
+    assert stats.followups_emitted == 1
+    assert halt.halt_id in tracker.followed_up
+
+
+def test_uncovered_halt_never_delivered_or_followed_up():
+    halt = _halt(symbol="ZZZZ")
+    universe = StubUniverse({})  # ZZZZ not covered
+    tracker = HaltTracker()
+    _deliver_halt(tracker, halt, universe, NewsCache())
+    assert halt.halt_id not in tracker.emitted_halts
+
+    stats = _run_followups(tracker, universe, _post_halt_pr_cache(symbol="ZZZZ"))
+    assert stats.followups_emitted == 0
+    assert tracker.followed_up == set()
+
+
+def test_dry_run_followup_posts_nothing_and_marks_nothing():
+    halt = _halt()
+    universe = StubUniverse({"VRDN": _meta()})
+    tracker = HaltTracker()
+    _deliver_halt(tracker, halt, universe, NewsCache())
+    stats = _run_followups(tracker, universe, _post_halt_pr_cache(), slack_mode="dry-run")
+    assert stats.followups_emitted == 0
+    assert tracker.followed_up == set()  # re-testable — no mark in dry-run
+
+
+def test_no_news_cache_is_noop():
+    halt = _halt()
+    universe = StubUniverse({"VRDN": _meta()})
+    tracker = HaltTracker()
+    _deliver_halt(tracker, halt, universe, NewsCache())
+    stats = _run_followups(tracker, universe, None)
+    assert stats.followups_emitted == 0
+    assert tracker.followed_up == set()

+"""Tests for the Slack delivery module."""
+from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch
+
+import pytest
+
+from src.coverage import TickerMeta
+from src.feeds.types import HaltEvent
+from src.news.types import NewsItem
+from src.slack import (
+    build_dm_blocks,
+    build_followup_blocks,
+    build_halt_blocks,
+    build_resume_blocks,
+    post_followup,
+    post_halt,
+    post_payload,
+    post_resume,
+    resolve_webhook_url,
+)
+
+
+def make_event(**overrides):
+    defaults = dict(
+        symbol="VRDN",
+        exchange="Nasdaq",
+        halt_date="2026-05-05",
+        halt_time="06:55:32",
+        reason_code="T1",
+        reason_description="News Pending",
+        name="Viridian Therapeutics",
+        last_price=14.06,
+        source="nasdaq_rss",
+    )
+    defaults.update(overrides)
+    return HaltEvent(**defaults)
+
+
+def make_meta(**overrides):
+    defaults = dict(
+        symbol="VRDN",
+        name="Viridian Therapeutics Inc",
+        sector="MedTech",
+        subsector="Diagnostics",
+    )
+    defaults.update(overrides)
+    return TickerMeta(**defaults)
+
+
+def test_halt_blocks_have_required_fields():
+    payload = build_halt_blocks(make_event(), make_meta())
+    assert "blocks" in payload
+    assert "text" in payload
+    assert payload["blocks"][0]["type"] == "section"
+    assert payload["blocks"][0]["text"]["type"] == "mrkdwn"
+
+
+def test_halt_blocks_include_ticker_and_name():
+    payload = build_halt_blocks(make_event(), make_meta())
+    text = payload["blocks"][0]["text"]["text"]
+    assert "*SA:* `VRDN`" in text
+    assert "Viridian Therapeutics Inc" in text
+
+
+def test_halt_blocks_use_no_entry_emoji():
+    payload = build_halt_blocks(make_event(), make_meta())
+    assert ":no_entry:" in payload["blocks"][0]["text"]["text"]
+
+
+def test_halt_blocks_include_reason():
+    payload = build_halt_blocks(make_event(), make_meta())
+    assert "T1 - News Pending" in payload["blocks"][0]["text"]["text"]
+
+
+def test_halt_blocks_include_sector():
+    payload = build_halt_blocks(make_event(), make_meta())
+    assert "Sector: MedTech / Diagnostics" in payload["blocks"][0]["text"]["text"]
+
+
+def test_halt_blocks_omit_sector_without_meta():
+    payload = build_halt_blocks(make_event(), None)
+    assert "Sector:" not in payload["blocks"][0]["text"]["text"]
+
+
+def test_halt_blocks_biotech_include_triage_cta():
+    """Biotech halts carry the copy-pasteable triage nudge (option A, 2026-06-16)."""
+    payload = build_halt_blocks(make_event(), make_meta(subsector="Biotech"))
+    text = payload["blocks"][0]["text"]["text"]
+    assert "Biotech halt — triage this name?" in text
+    assert "`triage VRDN`" in text
+
+
+def test_halt_blocks_non_biotech_omit_triage_cta():
+    payload = build_halt_blocks(make_event(), make_meta(subsector="Diagnostics"))
+    assert "triage this name" not in payload["blocks"][0]["text"]["text"]
+
+
+def test_halt_blocks_no_meta_omit_triage_cta():
+    payload = build_halt_blocks(make_event(), None)
+    assert "triage this name" not in payload["blocks"][0]["text"]["text"]
+
+
+def test_halt_blocks_include_note_context_when_provided():
+    payload = build_halt_blocks(
+        make_event(), make_meta(),
+        note_context="Note VRDN is scheduled to report earnings this morning",
+    )
+    text = payload["blocks"][0]["text"]["text"]
+    assert "_Note VRDN is scheduled to report earnings this morning_" in text
+
+
+def test_halt_blocks_omit_note_context_when_absent():
+    payload = build_halt_blocks(make_event(), make_meta())
+    text = payload["blocks"][0]["text"]["text"]
+    assert "Note " not in text
+
+
+def test_halt_blocks_include_price():
+    payload = build_halt_blocks(make_event(last_price=14.06), make_meta())
+    assert "$14.06" in payload["blocks"][0]["text"]["text"]
+
+
+def test_halt_blocks_handle_missing_price():
+    payload = build_halt_blocks(make_event(last_price=None), make_meta())
+    assert payload is not None
+
+
+def test_halt_fallback_no_markdown():
+    payload = build_halt_blocks(make_event(), make_meta())
+    fallback = payload["text"]
+    assert "*" not in fallback
+    assert "VRDN" in fallback
+
+
+def test_resume_blocks():
+    event = make_event(resume_trade_time="07:30:00")
+    payload = build_resume_blocks(event, make_meta())
+    text = payload["blocks"][0]["text"]["text"]
+    assert ":white_check_mark:" in text
+    assert "07:30 ET" in text
+    assert "06:55 ET" in text
+
+
+def make_news(**overrides):
+    defaults = dict(
+        source="prnewswire",
+        title="Viridian reports positive Phase 3 topline results",
+        body="b",
+        url="https://prn.test/vrdn",
+        published_at="2026-05-05T11:10:00+00:00",  # 07:10 ET
+        tickers=("VRDN",),
+    )
+    defaults.update(overrides)
+    return NewsItem(**defaults)
+
+
+def test_followup_blocks_have_required_fields():
+    payload = build_followup_blocks(
+        make_event(halt_time="07:00:00"), make_news(), make_meta())
+    assert "blocks" in payload
+    assert "text" in payload
+    assert payload["blocks"][0]["type"] == "section"
+    assert payload["blocks"][0]["text"]["type"] == "mrkdwn"
+
+
+def test_followup_blocks_headline_ticker_and_source():
+    payload = build_followup_blocks(
+        make_event(halt_time="07:00:00"), make_news(), make_meta())
+    text = payload["blocks"][0]["text"]["text"]
+    assert ":newspaper:" in text
+    assert "*SA:* `VRDN` — Follow-up: Viridian reports positive Phase 3 topline" in text
+    assert "07:10 ET 5/05/26" in text
+    assert "Follows the 07:00 ET halt on `VRDN`" in text
+    assert "PR Newswire press release" in text
+    assert "Sector: MedTech / Diagnostics" in text
+
+
+def test_followup_blocks_include_price():
+    payload = build_followup_blocks(make_event(last_price=14.06), make_news(), make_meta())
+    assert "$14.06" in payload["blocks"][0]["text"]["text"]
+
+
+def test_followup_blocks_handle_missing_price():
+    payload = build_followup_blocks(make_event(last_price=None), make_news(), make_meta())
+    assert "$" not in payload["blocks"][0]["text"]["text"]
+
+
+def test_followup_blocks_omit_sector_without_meta():
+    payload = build_followup_blocks(make_event(), make_news(), None)
+    assert "Sector:" not in payload["blocks"][0]["text"]["text"]
+
+
+def test_followup_fallback_no_markdown():
+    payload = build_followup_blocks(make_event(), make_news(), make_meta())
+    assert "*" not in payload["text"]
+    assert "VRDN" in payload["text"]
+
+
+@patch("src.slack.requests.post")
+def test_post_followup_dry_run_no_post(mock_post):
+    payload = post_followup(make_event(), make_news(), make_meta(), dry_run=True)
+    mock_post.assert_not_called()
+    assert payload is not None
+
+
+@patch("src.slack.requests.post")
+def test_post_followup_live(mock_post):
+    mock_post.return_value = MagicMock(status_code=200)
+    mock_post.return_value.raise_for_status = MagicMock()
+    post_followup(make_event(), make_news(), make_meta(), webhook_url="https://test")
+    mock_post.assert_called_once()
+
+
+def test_dm_warning():
+    payload = build_dm_blocks("test message")
+    assert ":warning:" in payload["blocks"][0]["text"]["text"]
+
+
+def test_dm_error():
+    payload = build_dm_blocks("died", level="error")
+    assert ":x:" in payload["blocks"][0]["text"]["text"]
+
+
+def test_dm_ok():
+    payload = build_dm_blocks("ok", level="ok")
+    assert ":white_check_mark:" in payload["blocks"][0]["text"]["text"]
+
+
+def test_resolve_webhook_from_env(monkeypatch):
+    monkeypatch.setenv("SLACK_WEBHOOK_STREET_ACCOUNT", "https://hooks.slack.com/services/TEST")
+    assert resolve_webhook_url() == "https://hooks.slack.com/services/TEST"
+
+
+def test_resolve_webhook_strips_whitespace(monkeypatch):
+    monkeypatch.setenv("SLACK_WEBHOOK_STREET_ACCOUNT", "  https://hooks.slack.com/services/TEST\n")
+    assert resolve_webhook_url() == "https://hooks.slack.com/services/TEST"
+
+
+def test_resolve_webhook_from_file(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
+    monkeypatch.delenv("SLACK_WEBHOOK_STREET_ACCOUNT", raising=False)
+    f = tmp_path / "webhook.txt"
+    f.write_text("https://hooks.slack.com/services/FILE\n")
+    assert resolve_webhook_url(file_path=f) == "https://hooks.slack.com/services/FILE"
+
+
+def test_resolve_webhook_raises_when_unset(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
+    monkeypatch.delenv("SLACK_WEBHOOK_STREET_ACCOUNT", raising=False)
+    f = tmp_path / "nonexistent.txt"
+    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match="No Slack webhook"):
+        resolve_webhook_url(file_path=f)
+
+
+@patch("src.slack.requests.post")
+def test_post_payload_calls_requests(mock_post):
+    mock_post.return_value = MagicMock(status_code=200)
+    mock_post.return_value.raise_for_status = MagicMock()
+    post_payload({"text": "hi"}, webhook_url="https://test")
+    mock_post.assert_called_once()
+
+
+@patch("src.slack.requests.post")
+def test_post_halt_dry_run_no_post(mock_post):
+    payload = post_halt(make_event(), make_meta(), dry_run=True)
+    mock_post.assert_not_called()
+    assert payload is not None
+
+
+@patch("src.slack.requests.post")
+def test_post_halt_live(mock_post):
+    mock_post.return_value = MagicMock(status_code=200)
+    mock_post.return_value.raise_for_status = MagicMock()
+    post_halt(make_event(), make_meta(), webhook_url="https://test")
+    mock_post.assert_called_once()
+
+
+@patch("src.slack.requests.post")
+def test_post_resume_dry_run_no_post(mock_post):
+    event = make_event(resume_trade_time="07:30:00")
+    payload = post_resume(event, make_meta(), dry_run=True)
+    mock_post.assert_not_called()

     assert out == []  # halt seen + resume already emitted
 
 
+def test_followed_up_round_trip(tmp_path):
+    """§7 follow-up markers persist so a restart never re-emits a follow-up."""
+    tracker = HaltTracker()
+    e = make_event("INSM", date="2026-04-07", time_="16:01:15")
+    list(tracker.ingest([e]))
+    tracker.followed_up.add(e.halt_id)
+    state.save(tracker, state_dir=tmp_path, day_key="2026-04-07")
+
+    rehydrated = state.load(state_dir=tmp_path, day_key="2026-04-07")
+    assert ("INSM", "2026-04-07", "16:01:15") in rehydrated.followed_up
+
+
+def test_emitted_halts_round_trip(tmp_path):
+    """Delivered-halt markers persist so the follow-up delivery gate survives
+    a restart (a follow-up only fires for a halt we actually delivered)."""
+    tracker = HaltTracker()
+    e = make_event("VRDN")
+    list(tracker.ingest([e]))
+    tracker.emitted_halts.add(e.halt_id)
+    state.save(tracker, state_dir=tmp_path, day_key="2026-05-05")
+
+    rehydrated = state.load(state_dir=tmp_path, day_key="2026-05-05")
+    assert ("VRDN", "2026-05-05", "06:55:32") in rehydrated.emitted_halts
+
+
+def test_load_old_file_without_new_fields(tmp_path):
+    """A pre-§7 state file (no emitted_halts / followed_up keys) rehydrates to
+    empty sets and still loads its halts."""
+    path = tmp_path / "dedup_state_2026-05-05.json"
+    path.write_text(
+        '{"schema_version": 1, "trading_day_et": "2026-05-05", '
+        '"halts": [["VRDN","2026-05-05","06:55:32"]], "resumes_emitted": []}'
+    )
+    fresh = state.load(state_dir=tmp_path, day_key="2026-05-05")
+    assert fresh.emitted_halts == set()
+    assert fresh.followed_up == set()
+    assert ("VRDN", "2026-05-05", "06:55:32") in fresh.seen_halts
+
+
 def test_cleanup_deletes_old_files(tmp_path):
     (tmp_path / "dedup_state_2020-01-01.json").write_text("{}")
     (tmp_path / "dedup_state_2099-12-31.json").write_text("{}")

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""Halt-event enrichment — the "Note:" context line on halt alerts (Phase 2).
     2	
     3	Per template-library.md §4–§5, SA halts often carry a one-line context preface:
     4	
     5	Slice 1 ("Note:" calendar context — LIVE):
     6	    Note ITGR is scheduled to report earnings this morning
     7	    Note AFRM is hosting an investor day today
     8	    Note WST is presenting at an investor conference today
     9	
    10	Slice 2B ("Follows {source} report that..." cross-ref — LIVE):
    11	    Follows PR Newswire press release that {title}
    12	    Follows Business Wire press release that {title}
    13	
    14	Cross-ref takes priority over calendar context — a coincident wire press
    15	release is a more specific signal than a generic "earnings today" note.
    16	
    17	PDUFA + clinical-readout context is deferred to a later slice. Pure-
    18	journalism feeds (FT/Bloomberg/Sky) are paywalled and out of scope.
    19	"""
    20	from __future__ import annotations
    21	
    22	from datetime import datetime, timezone
    23	from typing import Optional
    24	from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
    25	
    26	from .calendars import AnalystDayCalendar, AnalystDayEvent, EarningsCalendar, EarningsEvent
    27	from .feeds.types import HaltEvent
    28	from .news.cache import NewsCache
    29	from .news.types import NewsItem
    30	
    31	
    32	# Each entry is the full noun phrase including the article — "R&D" is read
    33	# "ar-en-dee" so it takes "an" despite starting with the consonant "R", which
    34	# rules me out of using a simple first-letter vowel test.
    35	_ANALYST_DAY_PHRASES = {
    36	    "investor_day": "an investor day",
    37	    "analyst_day": "an analyst day",
    38	    "rd_day": "an R&D day",
    39	    "capital_markets_day": "a capital markets day",
    40	}
    41	
    42	_NEWS_SOURCE_LABELS = {
    43	    "prnewswire": "PR Newswire",
    44	    "businesswire": "Business Wire",
    45	    "globenewswire": "GlobeNewswire",
    46	}
    47	
    48	# Cap for the {title} portion of cross-ref notes. Real SA bodies typically
    49	# inline ~80-120 chars before "(see linked comment)". We don't render the
    50	# linked comment, so a slightly tighter cap keeps Slack lines readable.
    51	_CROSS_REF_TITLE_LIMIT = 140
    52	
    53	
    54	def build_note_context(
    55	    event: HaltEvent,
    56	    *,
    57	    earnings: Optional[EarningsCalendar] = None,
    58	    analyst_days: Optional[AnalystDayCalendar] = None,
    59	    news_cache: Optional[NewsCache] = None,
    60	) -> Optional[str]:
    61	    """Return the Note context string for a halt, or None if no enrichment applies.
    62	
    63	    Priority order:
    64	    1. News cross-ref (most specific — actual press release coincident with halt)
    65	    2. Earnings calendar (same-day match, AMC/BMO grammar)
    66	    3. Analyst-day calendar (same-day match)
    67	    """
    68	    if news_cache is not None:
    69	        cross_ref = _cross_ref_note(event, news_cache)
    70	        if cross_ref is not None:
    71	            return cross_ref
    72	
    73	    if earnings is not None:
    74	        ev = earnings.get(event.symbol, event.halt_date)
    75	        if ev is not None:
    76	            return _earnings_note(event, ev)
    77	
    78	    if analyst_days is not None:
    79	        ad = analyst_days.get(event.symbol, event.halt_date)
    80	        if ad is not None:
    81	            note = _analyst_day_note(event, ad)
    82	            if note is not None:
    83	                return note
    84	
    85	    return None
    86	
    87	
    88	def _cross_ref_note(halt: HaltEvent, cache: NewsCache) -> Optional[str]:
    89	    """Look up news items in the cache that match this halt by ticker + time.
    90	    If a match exists, render a 'Follows {source} press release that {title}' note."""
    91	    halt_dt_utc = _halt_dt_to_utc(halt)
    92	    if halt_dt_utc is None:
    93	        return None
    94	    matches = cache.lookup(halt.symbol, halt_dt_utc)
    95	    if not matches:
    96	        return None
    97	    item = matches[0]  # newest within the window
    98	    label = _NEWS_SOURCE_LABELS.get(item.source, item.source)
    99	    title = (item.title or "").strip()
   100	    if len(title) > _CROSS_REF_TITLE_LIMIT:
   101	        title = title[:_CROSS_REF_TITLE_LIMIT].rsplit(" ", 1)[0] + "…"
   102	    return f"Follows {label} press release: {title}"
   103	
   104	
   105	def find_followup_news(halt: HaltEvent, cache: NewsCache) -> Optional[NewsItem]:
   106	    """Return the breaking NewsItem that resolves a previously-halted name — the
   107	    §7 Follow-up trigger — or None if nothing qualifies.
   108	
   109	    Matching reuses the SAME ticker+time core as `_cross_ref_note`: the halt is
   110	    converted to UTC via `_halt_dt_to_utc`, then `NewsCache.lookup` does the
   111	    ticker index + time-window filter. The ONLY difference from the halt-time
   112	    cross-ref note is the window and tie-break:
   113	
   114	    - Window: news published AT/AFTER the halt (`lookback_minutes=0`, so the
   115	      lower bound is the halt instant), out to the cache's own retention window
   116	      (`lookforward_minutes=cache.window_minutes` — the same 60-min span
   117	      enrichment already uses). This is the PR that crosses *after* the
   118	      "halted, news pending" alert already fired.
   119	    - Tie-break: `lookup` returns newest-first, so the EARLIEST at/after the
   120	      halt is `matches[-1]` — the breaking PR that resolved the halt, not the
   121	      latest unrelated wire.
   122	
   123	    Returns None if no item matches or the matched item has no usable title
   124	    (rule: no headline → no follow-up, never a guess).
   125	    """
   126	    halt_dt_utc = _halt_dt_to_utc(halt)
   127	    if halt_dt_utc is None:
   128	        return None
   129	    matches = cache.lookup(
   130	        halt.symbol, halt_dt_utc,
   131	        lookback_minutes=0,
   132	        lookforward_minutes=cache.window_minutes,
   133	    )
   134	    if not matches:
   135	        return None
   136	    item = matches[-1]  # earliest at/after the halt = the breaking PR
   137	    if not (item.title or "").strip():
   138	        return None
   139	    return item
   140	
   141	
   142	def _halt_dt_to_utc(halt: HaltEvent) -> Optional[datetime]:
   143	    """Convert halt_date + halt_time (in ET) to a UTC datetime.
   144	
   145	    Halt feeds publish wall-clock ET time; converting to UTC requires DST
   146	    awareness. Use zoneinfo's America/New_York which handles EDT/EST splits
   147	    correctly. Returns None on malformed input rather than raising.
   148	    """
   149	    if not halt.halt_date or not halt.halt_time:
   150	        return None
   151	    parts = halt.halt_time.split(":")
   152	    if len(parts) < 2:
   153	        return None
   154	    try:
   155	        y, m, d = (int(x) for x in halt.halt_date.split("-"))
   156	        hh = int(parts[0])
   157	        mm = int(parts[1])
   158	        ss = int(float(parts[2])) if len(parts) >= 3 else 0
   159	    except (ValueError, IndexError):
   160	        return None
   161	    et = ZoneInfo("America/New_York")
   162	    try:
   163	        local = datetime(y, m, d, hh, mm, ss, tzinfo=et)
   164	    except ValueError:
   165	        return None
   166	    return local.astimezone(timezone.utc)
   167	
   168	
   169	def _earnings_note(halt: HaltEvent, ev: EarningsEvent) -> str:
   170	    when = _time_of_day_phrase(halt.halt_time, ev.event_hour)
   171	    return f"Note {halt.symbol} is scheduled to report earnings {when}"
   172	
   173	
   174	def _analyst_day_note(halt: HaltEvent, ev: AnalystDayEvent) -> Optional[str]:
   175	    if ev.event_type == "conference":
   176	        return f"Note {halt.symbol} is presenting at an investor conference today"
   177	    phrase = _ANALYST_DAY_PHRASES.get(ev.event_type)
   178	    if not phrase:
   179	        return None
   180	    return f"Note {halt.symbol} is hosting {phrase} today"

     1	"""Rolling in-memory cache of news items for halt cross-reference.
     2	
     3	Each session starts with an empty cache; first news poll populates it. Items
     4	are indexed by extracted ticker (one item can appear under multiple tickers).
     5	The cache evicts items older than `window_minutes` (default 60) on every
     6	ingest — items that age out can no longer cross-ref a halt because by then
     7	they're outside SA's typical lookback grammar.
     8	
     9	Not persisted across sessions: cross-ref is most valuable for halts within
    10	~60 min of the news, and the AM/PM session boundary is well outside that
    11	window.
    12	"""
    13	from __future__ import annotations
    14	
    15	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    16	from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
    17	from typing import Optional
    18	
    19	from .types import NewsItem
    20	
    21	
    22	def _parse_iso(ts: str) -> Optional[datetime]:
    23	    if not ts:
    24	        return None
    25	    try:
    26	        dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
    27	    except ValueError:
    28	        return None
    29	    if dt.tzinfo is None:
    30	        dt = dt.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    31	    return dt.astimezone(timezone.utc)
    32	
    33	
    34	@dataclass
    35	class NewsCache:
    36	    """In-memory rolling cache of NewsItems indexed by ticker.
    37	
    38	    Thread-safety: not thread-safe. The halt_monitor poll loop is single-
    39	    threaded so this is fine.
    40	    """
    41	
    42	    window_minutes: int = 60
    43	    _by_ticker: dict[str, list[NewsItem]] = field(default_factory=dict)
    44	    _seen_ids: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    45	
    46	    def ingest(self, items: list[NewsItem], *, now_utc: Optional[datetime] = None) -> int:
    47	        """Add new items to the cache, dedup by news_id, evict aged items.
    48	        Returns the count of newly-added items (excludes dupes)."""
    49	        now = now_utc or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    50	        added = 0
    51	        for item in items:
    52	            if not item.tickers:
    53	                continue
    54	            if item.news_id in self._seen_ids:
    55	                continue
    56	            self._seen_ids.add(item.news_id)
    57	            for ticker in item.tickers:
    58	                self._by_ticker.setdefault(ticker.upper(), []).append(item)
    59	            added += 1
    60	        self._evict_older_than(now - timedelta(minutes=self.window_minutes))
    61	        return added
    62	
    63	    def _evict_older_than(self, cutoff: datetime) -> None:
    64	        for ticker, lst in list(self._by_ticker.items()):
    65	            kept = [it for it in lst if (_parse_iso(it.published_at) or cutoff) >= cutoff]
    66	            if kept:
    67	                self._by_ticker[ticker] = kept
    68	            else:
    69	                del self._by_ticker[ticker]
    70	
    71	    def lookup(self, ticker: str, halt_dt_utc: datetime,
    72	               *, lookback_minutes: int = 60,
    73	               lookforward_minutes: int = 5) -> list[NewsItem]:
    74	        """Return news items for a ticker published within
    75	        [halt_dt - lookback, halt_dt + lookforward]. Sorted newest-first.
    76	
    77	        Lookforward is small (5 min) and exists to catch press releases that
    78	        cross the wire seconds AFTER a halt fires — common pattern when
    79	        a company files a halt request and issues PR concurrently.
    80	        """
    81	        items = self._by_ticker.get(ticker.upper(), [])
    82	        if not items:
    83	            return []
    84	        lo = halt_dt_utc - timedelta(minutes=lookback_minutes)
    85	        hi = halt_dt_utc + timedelta(minutes=lookforward_minutes)
    86	        matches: list[tuple[datetime, NewsItem]] = []
    87	        for it in items:
    88	            pub = _parse_iso(it.published_at)
    89	            if pub is None:
    90	                continue
    91	            if lo <= pub <= hi:
    92	                matches.append((pub, it))
    93	        matches.sort(key=lambda x: x[0], reverse=True)
    94	        return [m[1] for m in matches]
    95	
    96	    def __len__(self) -> int:
    97	        return len(self._seen_ids)
    98	
    99	    @property
   100	    def tickers_indexed(self) -> int:
   101	        return len(self._by_ticker)

     1	"""Persistent dedup state for sa-monitor halt-monitor.
     2	
     3	The halt-id dedup tracker (HaltTracker in src/dedup.py) is in-memory only.
     4	That works fine for one continuous run, but if the runner restarts mid-session
     5	(GH Actions job timeout, manual rerun, exception), every previously-seen halt
     6	re-emits as new on the next poll — which means re-posting them all to Slack.
     7	
     8	This module persists the halt-id set (and the resumes-emitted set) to disk
     9	between polls so a fresh start can rehydrate without spamming.
    10	
    11	State lives under `state/` keyed by trading day (ET). On boot, the runner
    12	reads today's file; on each poll, it writes the full set atomically. Stale
    13	files older than 7 days get cleaned up on boot to keep the dir tidy.
    14	
    15	File format: JSON
    16	  {
    17	    "trading_day_et": "2026-05-05",
    18	    "saved_at_utc": "2026-05-05T20:30:00+00:00",
    19	    "halts": [["VRDN","2026-05-05","06:55:32"], …],
    20	    "resumes_emitted": [["INSM","2026-04-07","16:01:15"], …],
    21	    "emitted_halts": [["VRDN","2026-05-05","06:55:32"], …],
    22	    "followed_up": [["INSM","2026-04-07","16:01:15"], …]
    23	  }
    24	"""
    25	from __future__ import annotations
    26	
    27	import datetime as dt
    28	import json
    29	import logging
    30	import os
    31	import tempfile
    32	from pathlib import Path
    33	
    34	from .dedup import HaltTracker
    35	from .feeds.types import HaltEvent
    36	
    37	log = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    38	
    39	REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    40	DEFAULT_STATE_DIR = REPO_ROOT / "state"
    41	ET = dt.timezone(dt.timedelta(hours=-4))  # EDT during DST; close enough for daily-rotation key
    42	
    43	
    44	def _today_et_key() -> str:
    45	    return dt.datetime.now(ET).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    46	
    47	
    48	def _state_file_for(day_key: str, state_dir: Path) -> Path:
    49	    return state_dir / f"dedup_state_{day_key}.json"
    50	
    51	
    52	def save(tracker: HaltTracker, *, state_dir: Path = DEFAULT_STATE_DIR,
    53	         day_key: str = "") -> Path:
    54	    """Persist the tracker's halt-id sets to disk atomically."""
    55	    state_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    56	    day_key = day_key or _today_et_key()
    57	    path = _state_file_for(day_key, state_dir)
    58	
    59	    payload = {
    60	        "schema_version": 1,
    61	        "trading_day_et": day_key,
    62	        "saved_at_utc": dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
    63	        "halts": [list(hid) for hid in tracker.seen_halts.keys()],
    64	        "resumes_emitted": [list(hid) for hid in tracker.resumes_emitted],
    65	        "emitted_halts": [list(hid) for hid in tracker.emitted_halts],
    66	        "followed_up": [list(hid) for hid in tracker.followed_up],
    67	    }
    68	
    69	    # Atomic write: tmp file → rename
    70	    fd, tmp_path = tempfile.mkstemp(dir=str(state_dir), prefix=".tmp_", suffix=".json")
    71	    try:
    72	        with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    73	            json.dump(payload, f, indent=2)
    74	        os.replace(tmp_path, path)
    75	    except Exception:
    76	        if os.path.exists(tmp_path):
    77	            os.unlink(tmp_path)
    78	        raise
    79	    return path
    80	
    81	
    82	def load(*, state_dir: Path = DEFAULT_STATE_DIR, day_key: str = "") -> HaltTracker:
    83	    """Rehydrate a tracker from today's state file (if any)."""
    84	    day_key = day_key or _today_et_key()
    85	    path = _state_file_for(day_key, state_dir)
    86	    tracker = HaltTracker()
    87	    if not path.exists():
    88	        log.info("state: no prior file at %s; starting fresh", path)
    89	        return tracker
    90	    try:
    91	        payload = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    92	    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
    93	        log.warning("state: failed to read %s (%s); starting fresh", path, exc)
    94	        return tracker
    95	
    96	    if payload.get("schema_version") != 1:
    97	        log.warning(
    98	            "state: unexpected schema_version %r in %s; starting fresh",
    99	            payload.get("schema_version"), path,
   100	        )
   101	        return tracker
   102	
   103	    halts = [tuple(h) for h in payload.get("halts", [])]
   104	    resumes = {tuple(r) for r in payload.get("resumes_emitted", [])}
   105	    # Additive fields (introduced after schema v1 shipped); older state files
   106	    # simply lack them and rehydrate to empty sets.
   107	    emitted_halts = {tuple(e) for e in payload.get("emitted_halts", [])}
   108	    followed_up = {tuple(f) for f in payload.get("followed_up", [])}
   109	
   110	    # Pre-fill seen_halts with placeholder HaltEvents — we only need the
   111	    # halt_ids to be populated so dedup works; the full event history isn't
   112	    # required for the runner's correctness.
   113	    for hid in halts:
   114	        symbol, halt_date, halt_time = hid
   115	        tracker.seen_halts[(symbol, halt_date, halt_time)] = HaltEvent(
   116	            symbol=symbol,
   117	            exchange="",
   118	            halt_date=halt_date,
   119	            halt_time=halt_time,
   120	            reason_code="",
   121	            reason_description="",
   122	            source="restored_from_state",
   123	        )
   124	    tracker.resumes_emitted = resumes
   125	    tracker.emitted_halts = emitted_halts
   126	    tracker.followed_up = followed_up
   127	    log.info(
   128	        "state: rehydrated from %s (%d halts, %d resumes, %d emitted, %d followed_up)",
   129	        path, len(halts), len(resumes), len(emitted_halts), len(followed_up),
   130	    )
   131	    return tracker
   132	
   133	
   134	def cleanup_old(*, state_dir: Path = DEFAULT_STATE_DIR, keep_days: int = 7) -> int:
   135	    """Delete state files older than keep_days. Returns count deleted."""
   136	    if not state_dir.exists():
   137	        return 0
   138	    cutoff = dt.datetime.now(ET) - dt.timedelta(days=keep_days)
   139	    cutoff_key = cutoff.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
   140	    deleted = 0
   141	    for f in state_dir.glob("dedup_state_*.json"):
   142	        try:
   143	            day = f.stem.replace("dedup_state_", "")
   144	            if day < cutoff_key:
   145	                f.unlink()
   146	                deleted += 1
   147	        except OSError:
   148	            pass
   149	    return deleted

     1	"""Halt-event deduplication.
     2	
     3	Both the Nasdaq RSS and NYSE CSV feeds report the same halt event (since the
     4	exchange tape consolidates both). Polling at 5s means each event re-appears
     5	in N consecutive polls until the feed rolls it off. We dedupe by halt-id
     6	`(symbol, halt_date, halt_time)` so the runner only fires one Slack message
     7	per real event, and one resume message per resume.
     8	
     9	State lives in memory for the duration of a single market-session run. A
    10	small persistence layer (writing seen-ids to disk between polls) is deferred
    11	to D6 — the end-to-end runner — so the script survives restart-mid-session.
    12	"""
    13	from __future__ import annotations
    14	
    15	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    16	from typing import Iterator
    17	
    18	from .feeds.types import HaltEvent
    19	
    20	# halt_id -> the most-recently-seen HaltEvent for that id (so resume info
    21	# accumulates as the feed publishes it).
    22	HaltId = tuple[str, str, str]
    23	
    24	
    25	@dataclass
    26	class HaltTracker:
    27	    """Tracks halt + resume events across feed polls.
    28	
    29	    Use:
    30	        tracker = HaltTracker()
    31	        for event in tracker.ingest(events_from_this_poll):
    32	            ... # only new halts and newly-published resumes come out
    33	    """
    34	
    35	    seen_halts: dict[HaltId, HaltEvent] = field(default_factory=dict)
    36	    resumes_emitted: set[HaltId] = field(default_factory=set)
    37	    # halt_ids we actually DELIVERED a halt alert for (passed the non-emit-code
    38	    # filter AND — in live mode — posted successfully). seen_halts records mere
    39	    # OBSERVATIONS; this records deliveries. A §7 Follow-up may only fire for a
    40	    # halt we delivered. Persisted so a restart preserves the gate.
    41	    emitted_halts: set[HaltId] = field(default_factory=set)
    42	    # halt_ids for which a §7 Follow-up alert has already fired — one per halt,
    43	    # ever. Persisted alongside seen_halts so a mid-session restart never
    44	    # re-emits a follow-up.
    45	    followed_up: set[HaltId] = field(default_factory=set)
    46	
    47	    def ingest(self, events: list[HaltEvent]) -> Iterator[tuple[str, HaltEvent]]:
    48	        """Diff `events` against state and yield (kind, event) for new ones.
    49	
    50	        kind ∈ {"halt", "resume"}.
    51	
    52	        - halt: first time we see this halt_id
    53	        - resume: previously-seen halt now has a resume_trade_time populated
    54	          AND we haven't emitted a resume for this halt_id yet
    55	        """
    56	        for event in events:
    57	            hid = event.halt_id
    58	            previous = self.seen_halts.get(hid)
    59	
    60	            if previous is None:
    61	                # First sighting — emit halt
    62	                self.seen_halts[hid] = event
    63	                yield ("halt", event)
    64	                # If the very first sighting already includes a resume time
    65	                # (rare — usually catch-up from a feed roll), also emit resume
    66	                if event.is_resumed and hid not in self.resumes_emitted:
    67	                    self.resumes_emitted.add(hid)
    68	                    yield ("resume", event)
    69	                continue
    70	
    71	            # Already seen — update stored event (resume info may have
    72	            # populated since the prior poll) and check whether resume
    73	            # is freshly-known.
    74	            self.seen_halts[hid] = event
    75	            if event.is_resumed and hid not in self.resumes_emitted:
    76	                self.resumes_emitted.add(hid)
    77	                yield ("resume", event)
    78	
    79	    def __len__(self) -> int:
    80	        return len(self.seen_halts)
    81	
    82	    def reset(self) -> None:
    83	        """Clear state — used at session boundary or for tests."""
    84	        self.seen_halts.clear()
    85	        self.resumes_emitted.clear()
    86	        self.emitted_halts.clear()
    87	        self.followed_up.clear()

     1	"""Tests for §7 Follow-up detection + emission (_emit_followups + delivery gate).
     2	
     3	Covers the correctness-sensitive lifecycle rules:
     4	- a follow-up fires ONLY for a halt sa-monitor actually DELIVERED an alert for
     5	  (emitted_halts), not one merely observed (seen_halts)
     6	- non-emit reason code (never alerted)      → no follow-up  (Bug 1)
     7	- live post_halt that failed (never posted)  → no follow-up  (Bug 2)
     8	- resolving PR already cached at halt time    → cross-ref note, NO duplicate
     9	  standalone follow-up                                        (Bug 3)
    10	- resolving PR that arrives in a LATER poll   → STILL fires a follow-up
    11	- one follow-up per halt, ever; no-match → nothing; dry-run posts+marks nothing
    12	"""

    34	def _halt(symbol="VRDN", reason_code="T1"):
    35	    # 07:00 ET on 2026-05-05 (EDT) == 11:00 UTC.
    36	    return HaltEvent(
    37	        symbol=symbol, exchange="Nasdaq", halt_date="2026-05-05",
    38	        halt_time="07:00:00", reason_code=reason_code,
    39	        reason_description="News Pending", name="Viridian Therapeutics",
    40	        last_price=14.06, source="nasdaq_rss",
    41	    )
    42	
    43	
    44	def _meta(symbol="VRDN"):
    45	    return TickerMeta(symbol=symbol, name="Viridian Therapeutics Inc",
    46	                      sector="Biopharma", subsector="Biotech")

    48	
    49	def _post_halt_pr_cache(pub="2026-05-05T11:10:00+00:00", symbol="VRDN",
    50	                        title="Viridian reports positive Phase 3 topline"):
    51	    cache = NewsCache()
    52	    now = datetime(2026, 5, 5, 11, 40, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    53	    cache.ingest([NewsItem(
    54	        source="prnewswire", title=title, body="b",
    55	        url=f"https://prn.test/{symbol}", published_at=pub, tickers=(symbol,),
    56	    )], now_utc=now)
    57	    return cache
    58	
    59	
    60	def _deliver_halt(tracker, event, universe, cache, *, slack_mode="off",
    61	                  include_non_emit=False, stats=None):
    62	    """Run the REAL halt-delivery path (tracker.ingest → _emit) so emitted_halts
    63	    is populated with production's exact delivery semantics."""
    64	    stats = stats or RunStats(started_at="t")
    65	    for kind, ev in tracker.ingest([event]):
    66	        _emit(kind, ev, universe, None, include_non_emit=include_non_emit,
    67	              stats=stats, slack_mode=slack_mode, news_cache=cache, tracker=tracker)
    68	    return stats
    69	
    70	
    71	def _run_followups(tracker, universe, cache, *, slack_mode="off"):
    72	    stats = RunStats(started_at="t")
    73	    _emit_followups(tracker, universe, cache, None,
    74	                    stats=stats, slack_mode=slack_mode)
    75	    return stats

    78	def test_delivered_covered_halt_then_later_pr_emits_followup(capsys):
    79	    halt = _halt()
    80	    universe = StubUniverse({"VRDN": _meta()})
    81	    tracker = HaltTracker()
    82	    _deliver_halt(tracker, halt, universe, NewsCache())  # delivered, no PR yet
    83	    assert halt.halt_id in tracker.emitted_halts
    84	
    85	    stats = _run_followups(tracker, universe, _post_halt_pr_cache())
    86	    assert stats.followups_emitted == 1
    87	    assert halt.halt_id in tracker.followed_up
    88	    out = capsys.readouterr().out
    89	    assert "Follow-up: Viridian reports positive Phase 3 topline" in out
    90	    assert "Follows the 07:00 ET halt on VRDN" in out

    93	def test_one_followup_per_halt_ever():

    99	
   100	    first = _run_followups(tracker, universe, cache)

   102	    assert first.followups_emitted == 1
   103	    assert second.followups_emitted == 0
   104	    assert len(tracker.followed_up) == 1

     1	"""Shared types for halt-feed parsers."""
     2	from __future__ import annotations
     3	
     4	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     5	from typing import Optional
     6	
     7	
     8	@dataclass(frozen=True)
     9	class HaltEvent:
    10	    """One halt event — same shape regardless of source feed.
    11	
    12	    Halt ID (for dedup): (symbol, halt_date, halt_time). Two halts on the same
    13	    symbol at the same exact second are pathological enough that we treat the
    14	    tuple as primary-key.
    15	    """
    16	
    17	    symbol: str
    18	    exchange: str  # "Nasdaq" | "NYSE" | "NYSE American" | "NYSE Arca" | etc.
    19	    halt_date: str  # ISO date "YYYY-MM-DD" in ET
    20	    halt_time: str  # "HH:MM:SS" in ET
    21	    reason_code: str  # canonical Nasdaq code (T1, LUDP, …)
    22	    reason_description: str  # human-readable
    23	    name: str = ""  # company name as published by the feed
    24	    last_price: Optional[float] = None  # may be None if feed doesn't include
    25	    resume_date: Optional[str] = None  # set when resume is published
    26	    resume_quote_time: Optional[str] = None
    27	    resume_trade_time: Optional[str] = None
    28	    source: str = ""  # "nasdaq_rss" | "nyse_csv"
    29	    raw: dict = field(default_factory=dict)  # full source row for debug
    30	
    31	    @property
    32	    def halt_id(self) -> tuple[str, str, str]:
    33	        """Dedup key — stable across re-fetches of the same event."""
    34	        return (self.symbol.upper(), self.halt_date, self.halt_time)
    35	
    36	    @property
    37	    def is_resumed(self) -> bool:
    38	        return self.resume_trade_time is not None
    39	
    40	    def __str__(self) -> str:
    41	        return f"{self.symbol} halt {self.halt_date} {self.halt_time} {self.reason_code} ({self.exchange})"

   360	def _post_health_heartbeat(stats: RunStats, slack_mode: str,

   450	    stats = RunStats(started_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds"))

   541	        for name, h in health.items():
   542	            stats.feed_health[name] = asdict(h)
   543	        log.info("run ended: polls=%d halts=%d resumes=%d errors=%d",
   544	                 stats.polls_completed, stats.halts_emitted,
   545	                 stats.resumes_emitted, stats.fetch_errors)
   546	        _post_health_heartbeat(stats, slack_mode, ended_with_error=ended_with_error)
   547	
   548	    return stats
   549	
   550	
   551	def main(argv: Optional[list[str]] = None) -> int:
   552	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="sa-monitor halt feed runner")
   553	    parser.add_argument("--once", action="store_true")
   554	    parser.add_argument("--duration", type=int, default=None)
   555	    parser.add_argument("--interval", type=int, default=5)
   556	    parser.add_argument(
   557	        "--log", type=Path,
   558	        default=Path("logs") / f"halt_monitor_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}.jsonl",
   559	    )
   560	    parser.add_argument("--include-non-emit", action="store_true")
   561	    parser.add_argument("--universe", type=Path, default=None)
   562	    parser.add_argument("--slack", choices=["off", "dry-run", "live"], default="off")
   563	    parser.add_argument("--no-persist", action="store_true",
   564	                        help="disable dedup-state persistence (default: enabled)")
   565	    parser.add_argument("--earnings-calendar", type=Path, default=None,
   566	                        help="path to earnings-agent upcoming_events.json (Phase 2 enrichment)")
   567	    parser.add_argument("--analyst-days-calendar", type=Path, default=None,
   568	                        help="path to analyst-days upcoming_events.json (Phase 2 enrichment)")
   569	    parser.add_argument("--news-cross-ref", action="store_true",
   570	                        help="enable PRN/BW/GNW news cross-ref enrichment (Phase 2 slice 2B)")
   571	    parser.add_argument("--news-window-minutes", type=int, default=60,
   572	                        help="lookback window for news cross-ref (default 60min)")
   573	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
   574	
   575	    stats = run(
   576	        once=args.once, duration_sec=args.duration, interval_sec=args.interval,
   577	        log_path=args.log, include_non_emit=args.include_non_emit,
   578	        universe_path=args.universe, slack_mode=args.slack,
   579	        persist_state=not args.no_persist,
   580	        earnings_calendar_path=args.earnings_calendar,
   581	        analyst_days_calendar_path=args.analyst_days_calendar,
   582	        enable_news_cross_ref=args.news_cross_ref,
   583	        news_window_minutes=args.news_window_minutes,
   584	    )
   585	    print(json.dumps(asdict(stats), indent=2))
   586	    return 0
   587	
   588	
   589	if __name__ == "__main__":
   590	    sys.exit(main())

    51	def resolve_webhook_url(env_var: str = ENV_VAR,

    53	    env = os.environ.get(env_var)

    64	def build_halt_blocks(event: HaltEvent, meta: Optional[TickerMeta],
    65	                      *, note_context: Optional[str] = None) -> dict:
    66	    name = (meta.name if meta else "") or event.name or event.symbol
    67	    date_short = _format_date_short(event.halt_date)
    68	    time_hhmm = _format_time_hhmm(event.halt_time)
    69	
    70	    headline = f":no_entry: *SA:* `{event.symbol}` — {name} halted, news pending"
    71	    if event.last_price is not None:
    72	        headline += f"  ·  {_format_price(event.last_price)}"
    73	
    74	    reason_line = (
    75	        f"`{time_hhmm} ET {date_short}`  ·  reason `{event.reason_code} - "
    76	        f"{event.reason_description}`"
    77	    )
    78	    if meta and meta.sector:
    79	        sector_str = meta.sector
    80	        if meta.subsector:
    81	            sector_str += f" / {meta.subsector}"
    82	        reason_line += f"  ·  Sector: {sector_str}"
    83	
    84	    source_line = f"_source: {event.source} ({event.exchange})_"
    85	    lines = [headline, reason_line]
    86	    if note_context:
    87	        lines.append(f"_{note_context}_")
    88	    # Lean halt->triage hand-off (option A): biotech halts carry a copy-pasteable
    89	    # triage nudge. See root biotech_catalyst_architecture_plan.md §3.
    90	    if meta and is_biotech(meta.subsector):
    91	        lines.append(biotech_triage_cta(event.symbol))
    92	    lines.append(source_line)
    93	    text_block = "\n".join(lines)
    94	    fallback = (
    95	        f"SA: {event.symbol} {name} halted, news pending "
    96	        f"({event.reason_code} - {event.reason_description})"
    97	    )
    98	    return {
    99	        "blocks": [
   100	            {
   101	                "type": "section",
   102	                "text": {"type": "mrkdwn", "text": text_block[:SLACK_SECTION_TEXT_LIMIT]},
   103	            }
   104	        ],
   105	        "text": fallback,
   106	    }
   107

   109	def build_resume_blocks(event: HaltEvent, meta: Optional[TickerMeta]) -> dict:
   110	    name = (meta.name if meta else "") or event.name or event.symbol

   113	    resume_hhmm = _format_time_hhmm(event.resume_trade_time or "")
   114

   116	        f":white_check_mark: *SA:* `{event.symbol}` — shares to resume trading "

   123	    )

   129	
   130	    source_line = f"_source: {event.source} ({event.exchange})_"

   147	def build_followup_blocks(halt: HaltEvent, item: NewsItem,
   148	                          meta: Optional[TickerMeta], *,
   149	                          last_price: Optional[float] = None) -> dict:
   150	    """Block Kit for a §7 Follow-up alert — the substantive news that broke on a
   151	    previously-halted covered name. Mirrors build_halt_blocks; references the
   152	    original halt in-body (no Slack threading over a webhook)."""

   155	    time_hhmm, date_short = followup_when(halt, item)
   156	    headline = followup_headline(item)
   157	
   158	    header = f":newspaper: *SA:* `{symbol}` — Follow-up: {headline}"

   160	    if price is not None:
   161	        header += f"  ·  {_format_price(price)}"
   162

   164	    detail = f"`{time_hhmm} ET {date_short}`  ·  Follows the {halt_hhmm} ET halt on `{symbol}`"
   165	    if meta and meta.sector:
   166	        sector_str = meta.sector
   167	        if meta.subsector:
   168	            sector_str += f" / {meta.subsector}"
   169	        detail += f"  ·  Sector: {sector_str}"
   170	
   171	    source_line = f"_source: {followup_source_label(item)} press release_"
   172	    text_block = "\n".join([header, detail, source_line])
   173	    fallback = f"SA: {symbol} Follow-up: {headline}"
   174	    return {
   175	        "blocks": [
   176	            {
   177	                "type": "section",
   178	                "text": {"type": "mrkdwn", "text": text_block[:SLACK_SECTION_TEXT_LIMIT]},
   179	            }
   180	        ],
   181	        "text": fallback,
   182	    }

   185	def build_dm_blocks(message: str, *, level: str = "warning") -> dict:
   186	    emoji = {"ok": ":white_check_mark:", "warning": ":warning:", "error": ":x:"}.get(
   187	        level, ":warning:"
   188	    )
   189	    text = f"{emoji} *sa-monitor*: {message}"
   190	    return {
   191	        "blocks": [{"type": "section", "text": {"type": "mrkdwn", "text": text}}],
   192	        "text": f"sa-monitor: {message}",
   193	    }

   195	
   196	def post_payload(payload: dict, *, webhook_url: Optional[str] = None,
   197	                 timeout: int = POST_TIMEOUT_SEC) -> requests.Response:
   198	    url = webhook_url or resolve_webhook_url()
   199	    headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
   200	    log.debug("slack: POST (url redacted)")
   201	    body = json.dumps(payload)
   202	    # Retry only transient transport errors (DNS-not-ready on wake, etc.); a
   203	    # successful-but-bad-status response is left to raise_for_status as before.
   204	    attempts = len(_RETRY_BACKOFF) + 1
   205	    last_exc = None
   206	    for i in range(attempts):
   207	        try:
   208	            resp = requests.post(url, headers=headers, data=body, timeout=timeout)
   209	            break
   210	        except requests.exceptions.RequestException as exc:
   211	            last_exc = exc
   212	            if i == attempts - 1:
   213	                raise
   214	            delay = _RETRY_BACKOFF[i]
   215	            log.warning("slack: POST attempt %d/%d failed (%s); retrying in %ds",
   216	                        i + 1, attempts, exc, delay)
   217	            if not os.environ.get("PYTEST_CURRENT_TEST"):
   218	                time.sleep(delay)
   219	    resp.raise_for_status()
   220	    return resp
   221	
   222	
   223	def post_halt(event: HaltEvent, meta: Optional[TickerMeta], *,
   224	              webhook_url: Optional[str] = None,
   225	              dry_run: bool = False,
   226	              note_context: Optional[str] = None) -> Optional[dict]:
   227	    payload = build_halt_blocks(event, meta, note_context=note_context)
   228	    if dry_run:
   229	        log.info("slack[dry-run] halt payload for %s:\n%s", event.symbol,
   230	                 json.dumps(payload, indent=2))
   231	        return payload
   232	    post_payload(payload, webhook_url=webhook_url)
   233	    return payload
   234

     1	"""Render halt and resume events to the sa-monitor Slack/stdout template.
     2	
     3	Template grammar lives in template-library.md §3 (basic halt) and §6 (resume).
     4	We use a tightened sa-monitor variant — see those sections — that adds the
     5	exchange-feed reason code (which SA does not surface) and the elapsed-halt
     6	duration on resume (which we can compute from the feed timestamps).
     7	
     8	For Phase 1 D4: this module returns plain text strings for stdout printing.
     9	D5 will switch to Slack Block Kit per HEALTH_REPORTING.md §4.1 conventions.
    10	"""
    11	from __future__ import annotations
    12	
    13	from datetime import datetime, timezone
    14	from typing import Optional
    15	from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
    16	
    17	from .feeds.types import HaltEvent
    18	from .news.types import NewsItem
    19	
    20	_ET = ZoneInfo("America/New_York")
    21	
    22	# Presentation labels for news-wire sources. Mirrors enrichment._NEWS_SOURCE_LABELS
    23	# (kept local so template.py has no upward dependency on enrichment).
    24	_NEWS_SOURCE_LABELS = {
    25	    "prnewswire": "PR Newswire",
    26	    "businesswire": "Business Wire",
    27	    "globenewswire": "GlobeNewswire",
    28	}
    29	
    30	# Cap for the {headline} portion of a §7 Follow-up. SA follow-up subjects run
    31	# long; this keeps the Slack line readable while preserving the substance.
    32	_FOLLOWUP_HEADLINE_LIMIT = 200
    33	
    34	
    35	def _format_date_short(iso_date: str) -> str:
    36	    """ '2026-05-05' -> '5/05/26' (matches SA's body grammar). """
    37	    try:
    38	        dt = datetime.strptime(iso_date, "%Y-%m-%d")
    39	        return f"{dt.month}/{dt.day:02d}/{dt.year % 100:02d}"
    40	    except (ValueError, TypeError):
    41	        return iso_date
    42	
    43	
    44	def _format_time_hhmm(time_str: str) -> str:
    45	    """ '06:56:32' -> '06:56'. Resume notifications use HH:MM in SA's grammar. """
    46	    if not time_str:
    47	        return ""
    48	    parts = time_str.split(":")
    49	    if len(parts) >= 2:
    50	        return f"{parts[0]}:{parts[1]}"
    51	    return time_str
    52	
    53	
    54	def _format_price(price: Optional[float]) -> str:
    55	    """Format price as '$X.XX' or empty if None."""
    56	    if price is None:
    57	        return ""
    58	    return f"${price:.2f}"
    59	
    60	
    61	def _ticker_display_name(event: HaltEvent, name_override: str = "") -> str:
    62	    """Prefer name from sa_monitor_universe.json (override) over feed name."""
    63	    return (name_override or event.name or event.symbol).strip()
    64	
    65	
    66	def is_biotech(subsector: str) -> bool:
    67	    """True when the name's CM subsector is Biotech (case/space-insensitive)."""
    68	    return (subsector or "").strip().casefold() == "biotech"
    69	
    70	
    71	def biotech_triage_cta(symbol: str) -> str:
    72	    """Lean halt->triage hand-off nudge for a biotech halt (option A, 2026-06-16).
    73	
    74	    sa-monitor can't auto-invoke the Claude-driven biotech_triage, so instead of
    75	    pretending to trigger it we surface a copy-pasteable command. See the root
    76	    biotech_catalyst_architecture_plan.md (§3) for why this is human-mediated.
    77	    """
    78	    return f":dna: Biotech halt — triage this name?  ->  `triage {symbol}`"
    79	
    80	
    81	def render_halt(event: HaltEvent, *, sector: str = "", subsector: str = "",
    82	                name_override: str = "", note_context: Optional[str] = None) -> str:
    83	    """Render a halt notification — sa-monitor variant.
    84	
    85	    Phase 1 v0 template (per template-library.md §3):
    86	
    87	        SA: TICKER [Company halted, news pending]
    88	        HH:MM ET M/DD/YY [TICKER] halted at $X.XX, reason code [CODE - description]
    89	        Note TICKER is scheduled to report earnings this morning   <-- Phase 2 enrichment
    90	        Sector: {sector} / {subsector}
    91	    """
    92	    name = _ticker_display_name(event, name_override)
    93	    date_short = _format_date_short(event.halt_date)
    94	    time_hhmm = _format_time_hhmm(event.halt_time)
    95	    price_str = _format_price(event.last_price)
    96	
    97	    headline = f"SA: {event.symbol} [{name} halted, news pending]"
    98	
    99	    code_block = f"[{event.reason_code} - {event.reason_description}]"
   100	
   101	    if price_str:
   102	        body_line = (
   103	            f"{time_hhmm} ET {date_short} [{event.symbol}] halted at {price_str}, "
   104	            f"reason code {code_block}"
   105	        )
   106	    else:
   107	        body_line = (
   108	            f"{time_hhmm} ET {date_short} [{event.symbol}] halted, reason code "
   109	            f"{code_block}"
   110	        )
   111	
   112	    sector_line = ""
   113	    if sector:
   114	        sector_line = f"Sector: {sector}"
   115	        if subsector:
   116	            sector_line += f" / {subsector}"
   117	
   118	    parts = [headline, body_line]
   119	    if note_context:
   120	        parts.append(note_context)
   121	    if sector_line:
   122	        parts.append(sector_line)
   123	    if is_biotech(subsector):
   124	        parts.append(biotech_triage_cta(event.symbol))
   125	    parts.append(f"Source: {event.source} ({event.exchange})")
   126	    return "\n".join(parts)
   127	
   128	
   129	def _iso_utc_to_et(iso_ts: str) -> Optional[datetime]:
   130	    """ISO-8601 UTC timestamp -> ET datetime; None on malformed input."""
   131	    if not iso_ts:
   132	        return None
   133	    try:
   134	        dt = datetime.fromisoformat(iso_ts.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
   135	    except ValueError:
   136	        return None
   137	    if dt.tzinfo is None:
   138	        dt = dt.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
   139	    return dt.astimezone(_ET)
   140	
   141	
   142	def followup_when(halt: HaltEvent, item: NewsItem) -> tuple[str, str]:
   143	    """(time_hhmm, date_short) for a §7 Follow-up header.
   144	
   145	    The follow-up crosses when the news breaks, so the header stamps the news
   146	    item's publish time in ET. Falls back to the halt's own timestamp if the
   147	    item has no parseable publish time. Shared by render_followup + Slack so
   148	    both render the identical header.
   149	    """
   150	    et = _iso_utc_to_et(item.published_at)
   151	    if et is not None:
   152	        return (f"{et.hour:02d}:{et.minute:02d}",
   153	                f"{et.month}/{et.day:02d}/{et.year % 100:02d}")
   154	    return (_format_time_hhmm(halt.halt_time), _format_date_short(halt.halt_date))
   155	
   156	
   157	def followup_headline(item: NewsItem) -> str:
   158	    """The substantive headline for a §7 Follow-up, capped for readability."""
   159	    headline = (item.title or "").strip()
   160	    if len(headline) > _FOLLOWUP_HEADLINE_LIMIT:
   161	        headline = headline[:_FOLLOWUP_HEADLINE_LIMIT].rsplit(" ", 1)[0] + "…"
   162	    return headline
   163	
   164	
   165	def followup_source_label(item: NewsItem) -> str:
   166	    """Pretty wire-source label (e.g. 'PR Newswire') for a §7 Follow-up."""
   167	    return _NEWS_SOURCE_LABELS.get(item.source, item.source)
   168	
   169	
   170	def render_followup(halt: HaltEvent, item: NewsItem, *, sector: str = "",
   171	                    subsector: str = "", last_price: Optional[float] = None) -> str:
   172	    """Render a §7 Follow-up alert — the substantive news that broke on a
   173	    previously-halted covered name (template-library.md §7, webhook variant).
   174	
   175	        HH:MM ET M/DD/YY [StreetAccount] TICKER Follow-up: {headline} ($X.XX)
   176	        Follows the HH:MM ET halt on TICKER
   177	        Sector: {sector} / {subsector}
   178	        Source: {Wire} press release
   179	
   180	    sa-monitor posts via webhook (no Slack threading), so the original halt is
   181	    referenced in-body rather than via SA's archive block. Price is the halt's
   182	    last_price unless a fresher one is supplied.
   183	    """
   184	    symbol = halt.symbol
   185	    time_hhmm, date_short = followup_when(halt, item)
   186	    headline = followup_headline(item)
   187	
   188	    price = last_price if last_price is not None else halt.last_price
   189	    price_str = _format_price(price)
   190	
   191	    header = (
   192	        f"{time_hhmm} ET {date_short} [StreetAccount] {symbol} "
   193	        f"Follow-up: {headline}"
   194	    )
   195	    if price_str:
   196	        header += f" ({price_str})"
   197	
   198	    halt_hhmm = _format_time_hhmm(halt.halt_time)
   199	    back_ref = f"Follows the {halt_hhmm} ET halt on {symbol}"
   200	
   201	    parts = [header, back_ref]
   202	    if sector:
   203	        sector_line = f"Sector: {sector}"
   204	        if subsector:
   205	            sector_line += f" / {subsector}"
   206	        parts.append(sector_line)
   207	    parts.append(f"Source: {followup_source_label(item)} press release")
   208	    return "\n".join(parts)
   209	
   210	
   211	def render_resume(event: HaltEvent, *, sector: str = "", subsector: str = "",
   212	                  name_override: str = "") -> str:
   213	    """Render a resume notification — sa-monitor variant.
   214	
   215	    Phase 1 v0 template (per template-library.md §6):
   216	
   217	        SA: TICKER [shares to resume trading at HH:MM ET]
   218	        HH:MM ET M/DD/YY [TICKER] resume scheduled at HH:MM ET, originally halted at HH:MM ET
   219	        Sector: {sector} / {subsector}
   220	
   221	    Halt duration is omitted in v0 — it's computed in D6 with full timestamp
   222	    parsing once we know how the exchange feeds publish resume timestamps.
   223	    """
   224	    name = _ticker_display_name(event, name_override)
   225	    date_short = _format_date_short(event.halt_date)
   226	    halt_time_hhmm = _format_time_hhmm(event.halt_time)
   227	    resume_time_hhmm = _format_time_hhmm(event.resume_trade_time or "")
   228	
   229	    headline = f"SA: {event.symbol} [shares to resume trading at {resume_time_hhmm} ET]"
   230	
   231	    body_line = (
   232	        f"{resume_time_hhmm} ET {date_short} [{event.symbol}] resume scheduled "
   233	        f"at {resume_time_hhmm} ET, originally halted at {halt_time_hhmm} ET "
   234	        f"({event.reason_code} - {event.reason_description})"
   235	    )
   236	
   237	    sector_line = ""
   238	    if sector:
   239	        sector_line = f"Sector: {sector}"
   240	        if subsector:
   241	            sector_line += f" / {subsector}"
   242	
   243	    parts = [headline, body_line]
   244	    if sector_line:
   245	        parts.append(sector_line)

   272	# --- §7 Follow-up matcher (find_followup_news) -----------------------------
   273	# 07:00 ET on 2026-05-05 (EDT) == 11:00 UTC.
   274	
   275	
   276	def _followup_halt(symbol="VRDN"):
   277	    return HaltEvent(
   278	        symbol=symbol, exchange="Nasdaq", halt_date="2026-05-05",
   279	        halt_time="07:00:00", reason_code="T1", reason_description="News Pending",
   280	        name="Viridian Therapeutics", last_price=14.06, source="nasdaq_rss",
   281	    )
   282	
   283	
   284	def test_find_followup_matches_pr_after_halt():
   285	    halt = _followup_halt()
   286	    cache = NewsCache()
   287	    now = datetime(2026, 5, 5, 11, 10, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
   288	    cache.ingest([NewsItem(
   289	        source="prnewswire", title="Viridian reports positive Phase 3 topline",
   290	        body="b", url="https://prn.test/vrdn",
   291	        published_at="2026-05-05T11:10:00+00:00", tickers=("VRDN",),
   292	    )], now_utc=now)
   293	    item = find_followup_news(halt, cache)
   294	    assert item is not None
   295	    assert item.title == "Viridian reports positive Phase 3 topline"
   296	
   297	
   298	def test_find_followup_ignores_pr_before_halt():
   299	    """A PR that crossed BEFORE the halt is the cross-ref-note case, not a
   300	    follow-up — find_followup_news requires publish time AT/AFTER the halt."""
   301	    halt = _followup_halt()
   302	    cache = NewsCache()
   303	    now = datetime(2026, 5, 5, 11, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
   304	    cache.ingest([NewsItem(
   305	        source="prnewswire", title="pre-halt PR", body="b",
   306	        url="https://prn.test/pre", published_at="2026-05-05T10:50:00+00:00",
   307	        tickers=("VRDN",),
   308	    )], now_utc=now)
   309	    assert find_followup_news(halt, cache) is None
   310	
   311	
   312	def test_find_followup_picks_earliest_when_multiple():
   313	    """Among multiple post-halt PRs, the EARLIEST at/after the halt is the
   314	    breaking one that resolved it (tie-break opposite of the cross-ref note)."""
   315	    halt = _followup_halt()
   316	    cache = NewsCache()
   317	    now = datetime(2026, 5, 5, 11, 40, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
   318	    cache.ingest([
   319	        NewsItem(source="businesswire", title="later unrelated wire", body="b",
   320	                 url="https://x.test/late", published_at="2026-05-05T11:35:00+00:00",
   321	                 tickers=("VRDN",)),
   322	        NewsItem(source="prnewswire", title="breaking PR that resolved the halt",
   323	                 body="b", url="https://x.test/break",
   324	                 published_at="2026-05-05T11:05:00+00:00", tickers=("VRDN",)),
   325	    ], now_utc=now)
   326	    item = find_followup_news(halt, cache)
   327	    assert item.title == "breaking PR that resolved the halt"
   328	
   329	
   330	def test_find_followup_no_match_returns_none():
   331	    halt = _followup_halt()
   332	    assert find_followup_news(halt, NewsCache()) is None  # empty cache
   333	
   334

   342	    )], now_utc=now)
   343	    assert find_followup_news(halt, cache) is None

   346	def test_find_followup_reuses_halt_dt_conversion_returns_none_on_bad_halt():
   347	    """Malformed halt time flows through the shared _halt_dt_to_utc → None."""

   360	
   361	def test_earnings_takes_priority_over_analyst_day(tmp_path):
   362	    """If a ticker has BOTH an earnings event and an analyst-day on the same
   363	    date, prefer the earnings note — it's higher-signal for halt context."""
   364	    e = _write(tmp_path, "e.json", {

   100	def test_load_old_file_without_new_fields(tmp_path):
   101	    """A pre-§7 state file (no emitted_halts / followed_up keys) rehydrates to
   102	    empty sets and still loads its halts."""
   103	    path = tmp_path / "dedup_state_2026-05-05.json"
   104	    path.write_text(
   105	        '{"schema_version": 1, "trading_day_et": "2026-05-05", '
   106	        '"halts": [["VRDN","2026-05-05","06:55:32"]], "resumes_emitted": []}'
   107	    )
   108	    fresh = state.load(state_dir=tmp_path, day_key="2026-05-05")
   109	    assert fresh.emitted_halts == set()
   110	    assert fresh.followed_up == set()
   111	    assert ("VRDN", "2026-05-05", "06:55:32") in fresh.seen_halts
   112	
   113	
   114	def test_cleanup_deletes_old_files(tmp_path):
   115	    (tmp_path / "dedup_state_2020-01-01.json").write_text("{}")
   116	    (tmp_path / "dedup_state_2099-12-31.json").write_text("{}")
   117	    deleted = state.cleanup_old(state_dir=tmp_path, keep_days=7)
   118	    assert deleted == 1
   119	    assert (tmp_path / "dedup_state_2099-12-31.json").exists()
   120	    assert not (tmp_path / "dedup_state_2020-01-01.json").exists()

     2	
     3	Builds synthetic events, runs them through the dedup tracker, renders
     4	them via the template module, and confirms the output looks like the
     5	sa-monitor halt template documented in template-library.md §3 and §6.
     6	"""

    10	from src.template import render_halt, render_resume

    13	def test_synthetic_in_universe_halt_renders():
    14	    universe = Universe()
